PREFACE


This third and pial volume of the Magical Papyrus
inte a Demotic Glossary and various indices. Originally
‘we had the intention of adding pcan on the grammar
of the text, as is indicated by a few references made to it
in the notes of Vol. I., but various semen: have
prevented its fulfilment, We much regret the long delay


in the appearance of the present volume.
